## Alpha Futures — https://help.alpha-futures.com/en/articles/9508585-prohibited-trading-practices
- AUTO: Can I automate my trading?
- AUTO: Alpha Futures enforces the following guidelines on automation to maintain a fair and transparent trading environment:
- AUTO: Alpha Futures restricts the use of automated systems designed for high-frequency trading (HFT), particularly those resulting in over 100 trades per day.
- AUTO: Alpha Futures & Alpha Prime team are not interested in working with strategies taking 100+ trades per day, automated or not.
- AUTO: The use of AI, bots, and other automated trading mechanisms is strictly prohibited across all account types.
- AUTO: Semi-Automated Trading, such as a custom indicator that gives buy or sell signals, is permissible under the condition that the user manually places, monitors, and manages the trade while understanding the system and semi-automation's purpose.
- AUTO: Any form of hands-off, continuous day and night trading, or any other type of full automation is strictly forbidden.
- AUTO: "Tick or Micro Scalping" (trades less than 10 ticks AND less than 2 minutes)
- AUTO: Consistently just opening and closing positions less than 2 minutes to make profits without any plan, this is tick scalping.

- AUTO: لا يُسمح بـ EAs المباعة تجاريًا أو EAs المشفرة من طرف ثالث أو روبوتات التداول القائمة على الإشارات أو أي برنامج تداول آلي تم الحصول عليه من مصادر خارجية، بغض النظر عما إذا كان البرنامج مجانيًا أو مدفوعًا.
- AUTO: شراء EA من سوق عبر الإنترنت أو بائع تابع لجهة خارجية واستخدامها في حسابك.
- AUTO: استخدام EA تم تطويره أو ترميزه بواسطة شخص آخر، حتى لو تم تخصيصه لتفضيلات التداول الخاصة بك.
- AUTO: تنزيل وتشغيل EAs مجانًا من المنتديات عبر الإنترنت أو مواقع الويب أو مجتمعات التداول.
- AUTO: تشغيل EA الذي ينفذ الصفقات بناءً على الإشارات أو الاستراتيجيات التي تقدمها خدمة خارجية أو طرف ثالث.
- AUTO: استخدام EA تم تطويره بواسطة متداول آخر أو صديق أو مطور أو مجموعة تداول، بغض النظر عما إذا كنت قد دفعت مقابل ذلك أم لا.

- AUTO: Por qué no está permitido El HFT se basa en la velocidad y la infraestructura más que en el análisis del mercado y puede generar ventajas injustas, manipulación de precios o inestabilidad.
- AUTO: Sistemas automatizados que se centran en los retrasos en las cotizaciones, las discrepancias de precios efímeras o los tiempos de publicación, en lugar de ofrecer una visión del mercado.
- AUTO: Un EA lanza más de 50 operaciones por minuto en el EUR/USD, y cada operación se cierra en una fracción de segundo para corregir las discrepancias por debajo del pip entre el feed de la plataforma y un feed externo más rápido.
- AUTO: Un bot identifica los retrasos recurrentes en los feeds e introduce operaciones de forma sistemática antes de que se actualicen los precios retrasados.
- AUTO: Aclaración importante Una operación aislada que cierra inusualmente rápido no establece, por sí sola, una estrategia de HFT.
- AUTO: Por qué no está permitido El scalping de las garrapatas se basa en el ruido del mercado más que en el análisis o en una estrategia definida.
- AUTO: Se puede llevar a cabo de forma manual o mediante herramientas automatizadas.
- AUTO: La repetición de estas operaciones de corta duración basándose en un movimiento mínimo de precios constituye una práctica prohibida de scalping.
- AUTO: Aclaración importante El hecho de que una sola posición cierre en dos minutos porque se alcanzó inesperadamente su límite de pérdidas o obtención de beneficios no es necesariamente una prueba de una estrategia de scalping.

- AUTO: If you are unsure whether a trading strategy or automated tool is permitted, contact our support team before using it on your account.
- AUTO: High-Frequency Trading (HFT)
- AUTO: Use of Third-Party Expert Advisors (EAs)
- AUTO: Automated Multi-Trade Activity
- AUTO: High-Frequency Trading (HFT)
- AUTO: What it is Using Expert Advisors (EAs), bots, or algorithms to open and close large volumes of trades within fractions of a second, exploiting tiny, short-lived price movements or gaps in data feeds.
- AUTO: Why it is not allowed HFT relies on speed and infrastructure rather than market analysis and can create unfair advantages, price manipulation, or instability.
- AUTO: Automated systems targeting quote delays, short-lived price discrepancies, or feed timing rather than a market view.
- AUTO: An EA fires more than 50 trades per minute on EUR/USD, with each trade closed within a fraction of a second to target sub-pip discrepancies between the platform feed and a faster external feed.
- AUTO: A bot identifies recurring feed delays and systematically enters trades before the delayed price updates.
- AUTO: Important clarification An isolated trade that closes unusually quickly does not, by itself, establish an HFT strategy.
- AUTO: Why it is not allowed Tick scalping relies on market noise rather than analysis or a defined strategy.
- AUTO: It can be carried out manually or through automated tools.
- AUTO: Repeating these short-duration trades based on minimal price movement constitutes prohibited tick scalping.
- AUTO: Important clarification A single position closing within two minutes because its stop-loss or take-profit was reached unexpectedly is not necessarily evidence of a tick-scalping strategy.
- AUTO: Use of Third-Party Expert Advisors (EAs)
- AUTO: What it is: Using an Expert Advisor (EA), trading bot, or automated trading algorithm that you did not personally develop.
- AUTO: This includes commercially purchased EAs, rented EAs, downloaded bots, shared algorithms, or automated trading systems created by another individual or company.
- AUTO: Why it's not allowed: At Audacity Capital, traders are only permitted to use EAs that they have personally developed.
- AUTO: Commercially sold EAs, third-party coded EAs, signal-based trading bots, or any automated trading software obtained from external sources are not permitted, regardless of whether the software is free or paid.
- AUTO: Purchasing an EA from an online marketplace or third-party vendor and using it on your account.
- AUTO: Using an EA developed or coded by another person, even if it has been customised for your trading preferences.
- AUTO: Downloading and running free EAs from online forums, websites, or trading communities.
- AUTO: Using rented, shared, or subscription-based automated trading systems.
- AUTO: Running an EA that executes trades based on signals or strategies provided by an external service or third party.
- AUTO: Using an EA developed by another trader, friend, developer, or trading group, regardless of whether you paid for it.
- AUTO: Example: A trader purchases a commercially available scalping EA from a third-party website and installs it on their funded account.
- AUTO: Although the trader personally operates the software and controls its settings, the EA was not developed by them.
- AUTO: Traders may use their own personally developed EAs, provided that the EA complies with all other Audacity Capital trading rules and prohibited trading practices.
- AUTO: The fact that an EA is personally developed does not exempt it from restrictions on prohibited strategies or trading behaviour.

- AUTO: Parehong pinapayagan ang Expert Advisors at copy trading, kaya maaari mong i-automate ang iyong edge o salamin ang isang napatunayan na sistema.
- AUTO: Hindi pinapayagan ang pag-automate sa libreng kumpetisyon.
- AUTO: Mataas na Dalas na Kalakalan (HFT)
- AUTO: Ang paggamit ng High-Frequency Trading (HFT) bots, Expert Advisors (EA), at mga algorithm ng HFT ay hindi pinapayagan sa aming mga platform sa ilalim ng anumang sitwasyon.
- AUTO: Ang HFT ay nagsasangkot ng paggamit ng mga advanced na programa sa computer at mga mabilis na network upang gumawa ng maraming mga kalakalan nang napakabilis, madalas sa mga partisyon ng isang segundo.
- AUTO: Ipinagbabawal namin ang HFT dahil maaari itong humantong sa pagmamanipula sa merkado, magbigay ng hindi patas na bentahe, at maging sanhi ng kaw Kung matatagpuan ang sinumang negosyante na gumagamit ng HFT, lalabag nila ang aming Mga Tuntunin ng Paggamit at ibabawal ang pangangalakal sa aming platform.
- AUTO: Para sa mga halimbawa ng pang-abuso sa HFT, mangyaring sumangguni sa mga seksyon sa Trading on Deleted Data Feed at Trading on Deleted Trading Chart sa ibaba.
- AUTO: Kasama sa HFT ang mga kumplikadong diskarte sa pangangalakal na nagpapatupad ng maraming mga order sa isang maikling panahon.
- AUTO: Ang isang ipinagbabawal na diskarte sa HFT ay ang Latency Arbitrage, na nagsasamantala sa pagkaantala ng oras sa pagitan ng kapag ginawa ang isang kalakalan at kapag natanggap ang data ng merkado.

## Audacity Capital — https://audacitycapital.co.uk/tl/prohibited-trading-practices/
- AUTO: Mataas na Dalas na Kalakalan (HFT)
- AUTO: Paggamit ng Third-Party Expert Advisors (EA)
- AUTO: Mataas na Dalas na Kalakalan (HFT)
- AUTO: Ano ito Paggamit ng Expert Advisors (EA), bot, o algorithm upang buksan at isara ang malaking dami ng mga kalakalan sa loob ng mga partisyon ng isang segundo, samantalahin ang maliliit, maikling paggalaw ng presyo o mga puwang sa data feed.
- AUTO: Bakit hindi ito pinapayagan Ang HFT ay umaasa sa bilis at imprastraktura sa halip na pagsusuri sa merkado at maaaring lumikha ng hindi patas na pakinabang, pagmamanipula ng presyo Saklaw din ng paghihigpit na ito ang latency arbitrage: pagsasamantala sa mga pagkaantala sa pagitan ng pagpapatupad ng kalakalan at mga update sa data
- AUTO: Ang isang EA ay nagpapatakbo ng higit sa 50 trading bawat minuto sa EUR/USD, na ang bawat kalakalan ay sarado sa loob ng isang bahagi ng segundo upang i-target ang mga pagkakaiba-iba sa sub-pip sa pagitan ng feed ng platform at isang mas mabilis na panlabas na feed.
- AUTO: Kinikilala ng isang bot ang mga paulit-ulit na pagkaantala sa feed at sistematikong pumapasok sa mga trade bago ang naantala
- AUTO: Mahalagang paglilinaw Ang isang nakahiwalay na kalakalan na nagsasara nang hindi pangkaraniwang mabilis ay hindi, sa sarili nito, nagtatatag ng diskarte sa HFT.
- AUTO: Bakit hindi ito pinapayagan Ang tick scalping ay umaasa sa ingay sa merkado sa halip na pagsusuri o isang tinukoy na diskarte.
- AUTO: Mahalagang paglilinaw Ang isang solong posisyon na nagsasara sa loob ng dalawang minuto dahil hindi inaasahang naabot ang stop-loss o take-profit nito ay hindi kinakailangang katibayan ng isang diskarte sa tick-scalping.
- AUTO: Paggamit ng Third-Party Expert Advisors (EA)
- AUTO: Ano ito: Paggamit ng isang Expert Advisor (EA), trading bot, o awtomatikong algorithm ng pangangalakal na hindi mo personal na binuo.
- AUTO: Kabilang dito ang mga EA na binili sa komersyal, inuupahan na mga EA, na-download na bot, ibinahaging algorithm, o mga awtomatikong sistema ng pangangalakal na nilikha ng isa pang indibidwal
- AUTO: Bakit hindi ito pinapayagan: Sa Audacity Capital, pinahihintulutan lamang ng mga mangangalakal na gumamit ng mga EA na personal nilang binuo.
- AUTO: Hindi pinapayagan ang mga ibinebenta sa komersyal na mga EA, third-party na naka-code na mga EA, signal based trading bots, o anumang awtomatikong software sa pangangalakal na nakuha mula sa mga panlabas na mapagkukunan, anuman kung ang software ay libre o binayaran.
- AUTO: Pagbili ng isang EA mula sa isang online marketplace o third-party vendor at paggamit nito sa iyong account.
- AUTO: Paggamit ng isang EA na binuo o naka-code ng ibang tao, kahit na na-customize ito para sa iyong mga kagustuhan sa pangangalakal.

- AUTO: Pagpapatakbo ng isang EA na nagpapatupad ng mga kalakalan batay sa mga signal o diskarte na ibinigay ng isang panlabas na serbisyo o third party.
- AUTO: Paggamit ng isang EA na binuo ng isa pang negosyante, kaibigan, developer, o pangkat ng pangangalakal, anuman kung binayaran mo ito.

- AUTO: Ano ito Pag-mirror o pag-double ng mga posisyon ng ibang negosyante sa isang account, manu-mano o sa pamamagitan ng software ng copy-trading o isang awtomatikong tool.
- AUTO: Paggamit ng mga serbisyo sa pagkopya ng master-account o signal-provider.
- AUTO: Ikinokonekta ng isang negosyante ang account sa isang copy-trading platform na awtomatikong nagdukopya ng mga posisyon ng isang signal provider.
- AUTO: Awtomatikong naglalagay ng isang EA ang mga karagdagang order sa bawat nakapirming agwat habang gumagalaw pataas at pababa ang merkad
- AUTO: Mga awtomatikong script na naglalagay ng maraming mga kalakalan sa pagtanggap ng isang signal.

- AUTO: Suriin kung paano bumubuo ng kalakalan ang diskarte, kung paano nito pinamamamahalaan ang pagkakalantad, nakasalalay kung ito sa ibang tao o sa pag-automate ng third-party, at kung maaari itong mahulog sa ilalim ng higit sa isang paghihi Makipag-ugnay sa Audacity Capital Support kung ang anumang bahagi ng diskarte ay hindi malinaw.

- AUTO: Yüksek Frekanslı Ticaret (HFT)
- AUTO: Bu nedir Veri akışlarındaki küçük, kısa ömürlü fiyat hareketlerinden veya boşluklardan yararlanarak büyük hacimli işlemleri saniyenin kesirleri içinde açmak ve kapatmak için Uzman Danışmanlar (EAlar), botlar veya algoritmalar kullanmak.
- AUTO: Neden izin verilmiyor HFT, piyasa analizinden ziyade hız ve altyapıya dayanır ve haksız avantajlar, fiyat manipülasyonu veya istikrarsızlık yaratabilir.
- AUTO: Bir EA, EUR/USD'de dakikada 50'den fazla işlem gerçekleştirir ve her işlem, platform beslemesi ile daha hızlı bir harici besleme arasındaki alt pip tutarsızlıklarını hedeflemek için saniyenin çok kısa bir sürede kapatılır.
- AUTO: Bir bot, yinelenen besleme gecikmelerini tanımlar ve gecikmiş fiyat güncellemelerinden önce sistematik olarak işlemlere girer.
- AUTO: Önemli açıklama Alışılmadık derecede hızlı kapanan izole bir ticaret, kendi başına bir HFT stratejisi oluşturmaz.
- AUTO: Neden izin verilmiyor Tick scalping, analiz veya tanımlanmış bir stratejiden ziyade piyasa gürültüsüne dayanır.
- AUTO: Bu kısa süreli işlemlerin minimum fiyat hareketine dayalı olarak tekrarlanması yasaklanmış tik scalping anlamına gelir.
- AUTO: Önemli açıklama Kaybı durdurma veya kar alma işlemine beklenmedik bir şekilde ulaşıldığı için iki dakika içinde kapanan tek bir pozisyonun mutlaka bir tick scalping stratejisinin kanıtı değildir.
- AUTO: Üçüncü Taraf Uzman Danışmanların (EA) Kullanımı
- AUTO: Bu nedir: Kişisel olarak geliştirmediğiniz bir Uzman Danışman (EA), ticaret botu veya otomatik ticaret algoritması kullanmak.
- AUTO: Bu, ticari olarak satın alınan EA'ları, kiralık EA'ları, indirilen botları, paylaşılan algoritmaları veya başka bir kişi veya şirket tarafından oluşturulan otomatik ticaret sistemlerini içerir.
- AUTO: Neden izin verilmiyor: Audacity Capital'de, tüccarların yalnızca kişisel olarak geliştirdikleri EA'ları kullanmalarına izin verilir.
- AUTO: Ticari olarak satılan EA'lar, üçüncü taraf kodlu EA'lar, sinyal tabanlı ticaret botları veya harici kaynaklardan elde edilen herhangi bir otomatik ticaret yazılımına, yazılımın ücretsiz veya ücretli olmasına bakılmaksızın izin verilmez.
- AUTO: Bir çevrimiçi pazardan veya üçüncü taraf satıcıdan bir EA satın almak ve hesabınızda kullanmak.
- AUTO: İşlem tercihleriniz için özelleştirilmiş olsa bile, başka bir kişi tarafından geliştirilen veya kodlanmış bir EA kullanmak.
- AUTO: Çevrimiçi forumlardan, web sitelerinden veya ticaret topluluklarından ücretsiz EA'ları indirme ve çalıştırma.
- AUTO: Harici bir hizmet veya üçüncü taraf tarafından sağlanan sinyallere veya stratejilere dayalı alım satımları yürüten bir EA çalıştırmak.
- AUTO: Başka bir tüccar, arkadaş, geliştirici veya ticaret grubu tarafından geliştirilen bir EA'yı kullanmak, bunun için ödeme yapıp yapmadığınıza bakılmaksızın.
- AUTO: Örnek: Bir tüccar, üçüncü taraf bir web sitesinden ticari olarak temin edilebilen bir scalping EA satın alır ve finanse edilen hesabına yükler.
- AUTO: Tüccar yazılımı kişisel olarak çalıştırsa ve ayarlarını kontrol etse de, EA onlar tarafından geliştirilmemiştir.
- AUTO: Yatırımcılar, EA'nın diğer tüm Audacity Capital ticaret kurallarına ve yasaklanmış ticaret uygulamalarına uyması koşuluyla, kendi kişisel olarak geliştirilmiş EA'larını kullanabilirler.
- AUTO: Bir EA'nın kişisel olarak geliştirilmiş olması, onu yasaklanmış stratejiler veya ticaret davranışları üzerindeki kısıtlamalardan muaf tutmaz.
- AUTO: Piyasa yukarı ve aşağı hareket ettikçe EA, her sabit aralıkta otomatik olarak ek siparişler verir.
- AUTO: Diğer tüm EA ve ticaret kısıtlamaları geçerli olmaya devam ediyor.

- AUTO: Is Scalping Allowed?
- AUTO: Is Scalping Allowed?
- AUTO: Is Scalping Allowed?
- AUTO: ⚡ Yes, manual scalping is allowed.
- AUTO: Tick-scalping and HFT are not.
- AUTO: Scalping & Minimum Position Time
- AUTO: We welcome scalpers who profit from quick market moves.
- AUTO: However, we strictly distinguish between Legitimate Scalping (Skill) and Tick Scalping (Manipulation).
- AUTO: However, our Risk Team monitors for Tick Scalping behavior.
- AUTO: Trades that are consistently opened and closed in sub-second intervals (e.g., 0-5 seconds) are characteristic of High-Frequency Trading (HFT) and arbitrage bots.
- AUTO: If you are manually entering and exiting trades based on market structure, you have nothing to worry about.
- AUTO: Legitimate scalping typically lasts significantly longer than the latency of a data feed.

- AUTO: ¿Hay algo que me descalifique para unirme al Premium Programme?
- AUTO: Siempre que su trading sea legítimo y esté alineado con una gestión de riesgo adecuada, se ajuste a las condiciones reales del mercado y no constituya Prácticas de Trading Prohibidas , no tenemos motivos para limitar o restringir su estrategia de trading, ya sea trading discrecional, trading algorítmico o EAs.
- AUTO: Si tiene la intención de utilizar robots de trading (Expert Advisors – EAs), tenga en cuenta que si utiliza un EA de un tercero, es posible que otros traders ya estén utilizando el mismo EA y, por lo tanto, exactamente la misma estrategia.
- AUTO: Al utilizar un EA de terceros, corre potencialmente el riesgo de que se le deniegue la FTMO Account si excede la regla de asignación máxima de capital .
- AUTO: Si su EA causa hiperactividad en el servidor de la plataforma, es posible que le alertemos y le pidamos que ajuste la lógica del EA o los parámetros de su estrategia.

- AUTO: Si vous envisagez d’utiliser des robots de trading (Expert Advisors – EA), gardez à l’esprit que si vous utilisez un EA tiers, d’autres traders pourraient déjà utiliser ce même EA, et donc exactement la même stratégie.
- AUTO: En utilisant un EA tiers, vous vous exposez potentiellement au risque de vous voir refuser le FTMO Account si vous dépassez la règle d’allocation maximale de capital .

## FundedNext — https://help.fundednext.com/en/articles/8020351-what-are-the-restricted-prohibited-trading-strategies
- AUTO: Utilizing strategies that exploit Challenge Accounts will lead to the termination of a FundedNext Trader's Account, whether in the Challenge phase or while a FundedNext Account, "Pass Your Challenge", "Copy Trading Services" or "Signal Services" are also strictly prohibited, resulting in the denial of any FundedNext Accounts and a permanent ban from all FundedNext services.
- AUTO: High-Frequency Trading:
- AUTO: High-Frequency Trading (HFT) is a trading strategy characterized by the use of sophisticated expert advisors and high-speed telecommunication networks to execute an excessive number of trades within miliseconds to seconds.
- AUTO: While HFT may seem enticing due to its potential for rapid profit generation, it poses significant risks and can have detrimental effects on the market.
- AUTO: Here's why HFT is restricted on the FundedNext platform:
- AUTO: HFT trading can distort market prices and create artificial demand or supply.
- AUTO: By executing a large volume of trades within seconds, HFT traders can create false impressions of market activity, influencing other participants' decisions and leading to market manipulation.
- AUTO: Excessive trading volumes generated by high-frequency trading can disrupt market stability.
- AUTO: Note: If a trader receives a warning for hyperactivity and later engages in HFT (or vice versa), this may result in a more severe penalty, including account suspension.
- AUTO: Warnings are cumulative, and the system may immediately suspend accounts in cases of extreme or repeated Hyperactivity or HFT that causes major strain on our servers.
- AUTO: Copy Trading From Others:
- AUTO: At FundedNext, copy trading is allowed between FundedNext Challenge Accounts, under specific guidelines ​​to ensure fairness, transparency, and compliance with the platform’s policies.
- AUTO: However, Copy-Trading between multiple accounts not owned by the same individual, including those of relatives, family members, or friends, is strictly prohibited.
- AUTO: To get more details regarding Copy-Trading,  click here
- AUTO: Tick scalping refers to a trading strategy where traders aim to profit from small price fluctuations by executing a high volume of trades within a short time frame.
- AUTO: At FundedNext, limitations have been imposed on tick scalping as a result of its capacity for market manipulation and disruptive trading practices.
- AUTO: Example: A tick scalper uses automated trading algorithms to scalp ticks on instruments.
- AUTO: Consequences of Tick Scalping Violations
- AUTO: Depending on the severity of the violation, a Tick Scalping violation may result in immediate Account Termination or Permanent Termination from the Platform, with the Performance Reward or Refund denied or adjusted accordingly.
- AUTO: For example, a trader might continuously open consecutive "Buy" positions on a specific asset despite volatile market fluctuations or a clear downward trend, ignoring technical signals in the hope that a single market reversal will cover their exposure.
- AUTO: Note : If a trader receives a warning for hyperactivity and later engages in HFT (or vice versa), this may result in a more severe penalty, including account suspension.
- AUTO: Warnings are cumulative, and the system may immediately suspend accounts in cases of extreme or repeated Hyperactivity or HFT that causes major strain on our servers.
- AUTO: This includes external traders, trading groups, or automated services operated by another party on behalf of the account holder.

- AUTO: Why high-frequency trading is prohibited on Lucid Trading accounts, how HFT is defined, and which execution behaviors can trigger a rule violation.
- AUTO: What Is High-Frequency-Trading?
- AUTO: High-frequency trading (HFT) is a type of automated trading strategy that involves submitting and executing a high volume of trades within extremely short time frames, often measured in seconds or even milliseconds.
- AUTO: These strategies are typically powered by algorithms and designed to exploit minute price inefficiencies at speed.
- AUTO: What Happens If I Use HFT?
- AUTO: Lucid Trading employs automated risk detection systems to flag HFT activity.
- AUTO: If your account is identified as using high-frequency trading methods:
- AUTO: All profits generated from HFT activity will be removed
- AUTO: HFT strategies can result in hundreds of orders being placed within minutes, generating an unusually high load on platform infrastructure.
- AUTO: To maintain a reliable and equitable trading environment, HFT is strictly prohibited.

## The5ers — https://the5ers.com/faqs/prohibited-trading-practices/
- AUTO: High-frequency trading, in which the majority of trade durations span is measured within a few seconds or less.
- AUTO: Situation: A trader uses sophisticated algorithms to execute thousands of trades within milliseconds, taking advantage of small price movements in the market.
- AUTO: Situation: A trader opens manually  or uses automatic trading tools that open multiple trades at the same time, clearly showing a trader is not behind the strategy activity.
- AUTO: Trade coordination or copy trading with other traders or accounts
- AUTO: They share signals and strategies with each other, effectively copying each other’s trades to amplify their collective profits.
- AUTO: Expert Advisors that scalp during the  rollover  night  to take advantage of the price feed
- AUTO: Situation: A trader uses an Expert Advisor programmed to exploit price discrepancies during the rollover period when liquidity is lower.
- AUTO: The EA executes rapid trades to capitalize on small price differences between bid and ask prices.
- AUTO: EA from a third party, where other traders have the same trades open (copy trading)
- AUTO: Situation: A trader purchases an EA from a third-party provider without realizing that many other traders are already using the same EA with identical trading strategies, leading to saturation in the market and diminished effectiveness.
- AUTO: Using an EA from a provider where the trader does not own the sourc e code.
- AUTO: Situation: A trader subscribes to an EA service where they receive pre-built trading algorithms without access to the underlying source code.
- AUTO: They deploy these EAs without understanding how they operate or being able to customize them to their needs.
- AUTO: Using automated robots or Expert Advisors that cause the trading account to generate an excessive number of server requests per day through individual trades or pending orders being opened, modified, or closed, causing overload of the trading server.
- AUTO: Situation: A trader deploys a high-frequency Expert Advisor that continuously opens, modifies, and cancels pending orders across multiple instruments throughout the trading session, generating thousands of server-side requests per day and placing disproportionate load on the platform infrastructure.

## Top One Futures — https://help.toponefutures.com/en/articles/11680891-copy-trading-policy
- AUTO: Copy Trading Policy | Top One Futures Help Center Skip to main content
- AUTO: Our copy trading policy: same type and same size only, what counts as prohibited copy trading, correlated assets, and your responsibility when using a trade copier.
- AUTO: Top One Futures allows you to copy trades to manage multiple accounts efficiently.
- AUTO: However, to maintain risk integrity, copy trading is strictly limited to accounts of the exact same type and exact same size .
- AUTO: Mixing different account programs, sizes, or trying to bypass these rules through manual mirroring or correlated assets is a strict violation of our terms.
- AUTO: You can only copy trades between accounts that belong to the identical program and share the same size .
- AUTO: What counts as prohibited copy trading?
- AUTO: Copy trading violations are not just limited to software settings.
- AUTO: The following actions are classified as unauthorized copy trading :
- AUTO: Manual mirroring (delayed entries)
- AUTO: Manually entering the same trade across different account types or sizes to copy your own positions.
- AUTO: If you buy or sell the same instrument across mismatched accounts, even if it is minutes or an hour later, it is still classified as copy trading.
- AUTO: As long as the position is still active on your first account and the different account size or type goes in the same direction as well, it is classified as copy trading
- AUTO: Violating this Copy Trading Policy is treated as a prohibited trading practice.
- AUTO: What does "individual trading responsibility" mean for copy trading?
- AUTO: Following signal services, Telegram groups, Discord calls, or any shared trade alert system.
- AUTO: Note on copy trading between firms
- AUTO: Top One Futures allows copy trading between your own accounts at different firms, including through third-party platforms.
- AUTO: Signal following, group trading, or mirroring someone else’s trades is also strictly prohibited.
- AUTO: Your responsibility for copy trading adjustments and outcomes
- AUTO: Each trader is solely responsible for the configuration, supervision, and final outcomes of all trades executed via third-party copy trading software.
- AUTO: We cannot void losses, reset accounts, or provide refunds for trades originated by external software, regardless of whether the trader manually initiated the trade or not
- AUTO: Pre-trade requirement: it is crucial that you test and verify your copy trading settings thoroughly in a simulated environment before active trading to ensure accurate replication and alignment with your risk management strategy.
